Ablation studies also demonstrated the benefits of physics-guided regularization in terms of trend-following consistency, and physically implausible predictions. Explainability auditing also helped to focus attention in hydrodynamically active surf-zone regions and showed good agreement with the physically derived wave propagation behavior. In general, the proposed framework shows the promise of physics-guided video-based deep learning systems for long-term coastal wave monitoring that are cost-efficient and operationally feasible.

</details>


### [206] [ReSum: Synergizing LLM Reasoning and Summarization with Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13316)
*Xucong Wang,Ziyu Ma,Yong Wang,Shidong Yang,Hailang Huang,Renda Li,Pengkun Wang,Xiangxiang Chu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ReSum 是一种 RLVR 框架，通过让 LLM 在推理过程中进行自我总结来压缩和组织推理轨迹。它采用对比式自适应 rollout 机制和总结感知的优势函数，在提升性能约 4% 的同时将 rollout 长度缩短 18.6%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 RLVR 方法倾向于鼓励过长的推理 rollout，这会破坏推理连贯性并耗尽上下文预算。此外，现有长上下文组织方法依赖外部机制，而非让模型自主管理推理轨迹。因此，需要一种让模型能够自我压缩和组织推理过程的方法。

Method: 提出 ReSum 框架，核心包括：(1) 总结感知的自适应 rollout 机制——当模型自发触发自我总结时，掩盖总结短语以创建对比分支；在非总结位置则随机注入总结短语以创建匹配分支；(2) 总结感知的优势函数（advantage），用于在对比 rollout 轨迹之间进行更细粒度的比较。前期试验表明自我总结通过降低 token 级熵来稳定生成，且引入总结短语可显著缓解错误前缀传播。

Result: 在广泛实验上，ReSum 使性能平均提升约 4%，同时将 rollout 长度减少 18.6%。

Conclusion: ReSum 通过自我总结机制，成功在 RLVR 框架中实现了推理轨迹的自主压缩与组织，有效缓解了过长 rollout 导致的连贯性下降和上下文预算耗尽问题，在提升性能的同时显著降低了推理开销。

Abstract: Reinforcement Learning with Verifiable Rewards (RLVR) is a central technique for improving long-horizon reasoning in Large Language Models (LLMs). However, existing RLVR methods often encourage unnecessarily long reasoning rollouts, which can degrade reasoning coherence and exhaust the available context budget. Existing approaches to long-context organization often depend on external mechanisms to organize rollouts, rather than enabling the model to manage its own reasoning trajectory. To address this limitation, we propose ReSum, a novel RLVR framework that enables LLMs to compress and organize their reasoning trajectories through self-summarization. Our pilot studies show that self-summarization stabilizes generation by lowering token-level entropy, and that introducing a ``summarization'' phrase can substantially mitigate errors propagated from an incorrect rollout prefix. Motivated by these findings, ReSum adopts a summarization-aware adaptive rollout mechanism that contrastively evaluates whether self-summarization benefits the ongoing reasoning process. Specifically, when the model spontaneously triggers self-summarization, ReSum masks the summarization phrase to create a contrastive branch; for non-summarization positions, it instead randomly injects the phrase to create a matched branch. We further design a summarization-aware advantage to enable finer-grained comparison between contrastive rollout trajectories. Extensive experiments show that ReSum improves performance at an average of 4\% while reducing rollout length by 18.6\%.

</details>


### [207] [Can I Buy Your KV Cache?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13361)
*Luoyuan Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一种"预先计算并复用文档KV缓存"的机制，让AI Agent无需对同一文档重复执行prefill，可将计算成本降低9-50倍，并通过服务端托管消除数据传输成本，构建面向Agent时代的"预填充CDN"。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前全球AI Agent对同一文档反复从零执行prefill（Transformer推理中最昂贵的步骤），造成海量重复计算浪费。例如，一篇热门文档被8000万Agent读取时，重复prefill的成本高达150万美元，而实际复用成本仅需3万美元。这种"同一答案被计算百万次"的荒谬现状亟需解决。

Method: 核心方案：由发布者预先计算文档的KV缓存，其他Agent购买权限后直接加载该缓存跳过prefill。技术验证包括：(1) 在Qwen3-4B上验证加载预计算KV与从零prefill在24/24贪婪token及logits层面完全一致，零精度损失；(2) 对比传输KV缓存（失败，因KV几乎不可压缩，网络出口成本超过prefill节省）与服务端托管（成功，消除出口成本）；(3) 量化分析：对3774 token的文档，复用计算成本仅为prefill的1/49.7，现有API的0.1倍缓存读取费率可提供10倍折扣，剩余~50倍物理节省即为提供商利润空间。

Result: (1) Token-exact匹配：加载预计算KV与从零prefill完全一致；(2) Qwen3-4B上复用成本为prefill的1/9到1/50，且文本越长差距越大（prefill注意力复杂度O(L²)）；(3) 服务端托管可完全消除egress成本；(4) 经济模型：80M Agent读取一篇3774 token热门文档，prefill成本~$1.5M，复用仅~$0.03M（49.7倍节省），提供商的边际利润可达数百万美元每篇热门文档。

Conclusion: 提出Agent-native Prefill CDN框架，核心贡献是论证"计算一次、复用于众"在技术（token-exact、无损）和经济（巨额节省）上的双重可行性。开源问题包括无损KV压缩和跨方支付层。

Abstract: Right now, across the world, AI agents are repeating the same absurd act: to read one document, they each recompute it from scratch. Every agent re-runs prefill, the most compute-intensive step a large model takes, over identical text, only to rebuild a key-value (KV) cache identical to the one the agent before it just built. The same answer, computed a million times. We make a proposal that is almost offensively simple: compute it once. Let a publisher precompute a document's KV cache, and let every other agent buy the right to load it and skip prefill. It works, and it is token-exact: loading a precomputed KV and continuing matches prefilling from scratch (24/24 greedy tokens, and at the logits level), with no accuracy cost. On Qwen3-4B, reuse is 9-50x cheaper in compute than prefill, and the gap widens with length (prefill's attention scales with L^2), so a single reuse already pays it back. Then the part that matters: where the KV lives. Shipping it fails, because KV is nearly incompressible, so per-load egress costs more than the prefill it saves. Hosting it provider-side, exactly as production prompt-caching works, removes egress entirely. The size of the prize is set by our measured compute saving: serving one hot 3774-token document to 80M agents costs ~$1.5M to re-prefill but only ~$0.03M of reuse compute (49.7x less). The 0.1x cache-read tariff APIs charge passes a 10x discount to users while sitting inside this measured envelope, so the 10x is a floor that the measured ~50x compute saving clears, and the gap to the physical ~50x is provider margin: millions of dollars per popular document. We frame the resulting agent-native prefill CDN and leave lossless KV compression and a cross-party payment layer as the open problems.

</details>


### [208] [IterCAD: An Iterative Multimodal Agent for Visually-Grounded CAD Generation and Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13368)
*Tao Hu,Jiaxin Ai,Licheng Wen,Xueheng Li,Shu Zou,Siqi Li,Nianchen Deng,Xinyu Cai,Hongbin Zhou,Pinlong Cai,Daocheng Fu,Yu Yang,Hairong Zhang,Botian Shi,Xuemeng Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: IterCAD是一个统一的多模态智能体框架，用于闭环、交互式的CAD生成与编辑。它将任务建模为多模态智能体与可执行CAD沙盒之间的多轮交互，覆盖绘图转代码、文本转代码和交互式编辑三类任务，并通过渐进式SFT与几何感知强化学习进行优化，在代码可执行性和几何精度上显著超越现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自动化CAD方法主要依赖开环、一次性生成，与迭代式真实工作流程脱节，缺乏支持多轮闭环交互编辑的统一框架。

Method: 提出IterCAD框架，将CAD生成与编辑形式化为多模态智能体与可执行CAD沙盒的多轮交互；开发数据合成管线生成符合标准的多视图工程图、代码编辑任务及交互轨迹；采用渐进式监督微调（SFT）后进行几何感知强化学习，并设计可行前缀掩码（viable-prefix masking）策略以提升代码可执行性和几何保真度；提出IterCAD-Bench评测集以及Chamfer Distance Tolerance-Recall（CD-TR）曲线和AUC-TR指标，建立无幸存者偏差的代码有效性与几何精度统一标准。

Result: IterCAD在多个基准测试上取得极具竞争力的性能，在代码可执行性和几何精度方面显著优于现有方法，并在闭环迭代精化能力上表现卓越。

Conclusion: IterCAD通过闭环多模态智能体架构、几何感知强化学习以及新评测标准，有效弥合了自动化CAD生成与真实迭代实践之间的鸿沟，为交互式CAD智能设计提供了强有力的统一解决方案。

Abstract: Computer-Aided Design is pivotal in modern manufacturing, yet existing automated methods predominantly rely on open-loop, one-shot generation, creating a mismatch with iterative real-world practices. In this paper, we present IterCAD, a unified multimodal agent framework for closed-loop, interactive CAD generation and editing. We formulate the task as a multi-turn interaction between a multimodal agent and an executable CAD sandbox, covering three tasks: Drawing-to-Code, Text-to-Code, and Interactive Editing. To support this, we develop a data synthesis pipeline incorporating advanced industrial manufacturing features to generate standard-compliant multi-view engineering drawings, complex code-editing tasks, and high-fidelity interaction trajectories. We optimize the agent via progressive SFT followed by geometry-aware reinforcement learning with viable-prefix masking to enhance code executability and geometric fidelity. Finally, we introduce the IterCAD-Bench evaluation suite and propose the Chamfer Distance Tolerance-Recall (CD-TR) curve alongside its AUC-TR metric, establishing a survivor-bias-free standard that unifies code validity and geometric precision. Extensive experiments demonstrate that IterCAD achieves highly competitive performance across multiple benchmarks, significantly outperforming existing approaches in both code executability and geometric precision, while exhibiting superior capabilities in closed-loop iterative refinement.

</details>


### [209] [A Quantitative Experimental Repeated Measures Study of Training Dynamics in a Small Llama Style Language Model Under a Compute-Aware Token Budget](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13370)
*Joe Dwyer*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 在固定计算预算下训练小型Llama风格语言模型，通过重复测量设计分析21个训练区间的验证损失、困惑度、波动率及回撤行为，发现仅看最终指标会掩盖训练过程中的退化与不稳定现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有语言模型效率评估通常仅关注训练终点的性能指标，忽视了训练过程中的动态变化。本研究旨在揭示在计算受限条件下，训练轨迹中的非单调退化、回撤和不稳定性，论证仅依赖端点指标评估模型效率的局限性。

Method: 采用定量实验重复测量设计，在TinyStories语料库上对6个不同随机种子的4.26M参数Llama风格模型进行CPU全精度训练，目标预算约2000万token。在21个等距区间收集验证损失、验证困惑度、滚动波动率、回撤行为、尖峰行为等指标，共产生126个种子×区间观测值，使用重复测量ANOVA分析区间效应。

Result: ANOVA显示验证损失、验证困惑度和滚动波动率的区间效应具有统计显著性。验证损失从初始化的8.3552降至约400万token时的2.7996，但最终回升至3.9010；困惑度遵循相同先降后升模式。遥测数据显示反复的验证损失回撤，且在预设标准下未观察到稳定阶段。

Conclusion: 计算感知的语言模型评估应关注完整训练轨迹而非仅端点指标。在受限计算设置下，额外token暴露可能增加计算成本而未带来成比例的泛化增益；区间级遥测能够揭示最终指标所掩盖的不稳定性、性能退化和收益递减现象。

Abstract: This study examines training dynamics in a small Llama-style language model trained under a fixed, compute-constrained token budget. Rather than evaluating efficiency solely through endpoint performance, the study uses a quantitative experimental repeated measures design to analyze how validation loss, validation perplexity, rolling volatility, backslide behavior, spike behavior, and between-seed variability change across token-based training intervals. Six independent training runs were conducted on a 4.26-million-parameter model using the TinyStories corpus, CPU-based full-precision training, and a target budget of approximately 20 million cumulative training tokens. Metrics were collected across 21 intervals, producing 126 seed-by-interval observations. Repeated measures ANOVA showed statistically significant interval effects for validation loss, validation perplexity, and rolling volatility. Descriptive trajectories revealed rapid early improvement followed by non-monotonic degradation during later training intervals. Mean validation loss decreased from 8.3552 at initialization to 2.7996 near 4 million tokens, but increased to 3.9010 by the final checkpoint. Validation perplexity followed the same pattern, falling sharply early in training before rising later. Derived telemetry further showed recurrent validation-loss backslides and no interval-summary evidence of a stable phase under the predefined criteria. These findings suggest that compute-aware language model evaluation should examine training trajectories rather than endpoint metrics alone. In constrained compute settings, additional token exposure may increase computational cost without producing proportional generalization gains, and interval-level telemetry can reveal instability, regression, and diminishing returns that final metrics may obscure.

</details>


### [210] [MiniMax Sparse Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13392)
*Xunhao Lai,Weiqi Xu,Yufeng Yang,Qiaorui Chen,Yang Xu,Lunbin Zeng,Xiaolong Li,Haohai Sun,Haichao Zhu,Vito Zhang,Pengyu Zhao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: MiniMax 提出 MSA（MiniMax Sparse Attention），一种基于 GQA 的块级稀疏注意力机制。通过轻量 Index Branch 为每个 GQA group 独立选择 Top-k 关键块，Main Branch 仅对选中块做精确注意力计算。配合 GPU 协同设计的 kernel，在 109B 多模态模型上以 28.4 倍注意力计算量降低（1M 上下文），实现 prefill 14.2 倍、解码 7.6 倍 wall-clock 加速，同时保持与 GQA 相当的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前沿大模型在 agent 工作流、仓库级代码推理、持久记忆等场景中需要处理百万 token 级上下文，但 softmax 注意力的二次复杂度使其在部署规模上不可行。现有稀疏注意力方案往往在实现效率与模型性能之间难以兼得，亟需一种既保持强性能、又能在实际 GPU 上高效执行的稀疏注意力方案。

Method: MSA 在 GQA 基础上引入双分支设计：(1) Index Branch——轻量评分模块对 KV 块打分，按 GQA group 独立进行 Top-k 块选择，实现 group 级稀疏检索；(2) Main Branch——仅对选中的块执行精确的块稀疏注意力。协同设计方面，采用无需 exp 的 Top-k 选择与 KV-outer 稀疏注意力，优化块粒度访问下的张量核心利用率，实现 GPU 高效执行。

Result: 在 109B 参数原生多模态模型上，MSA 性能与 GQA 持平，同时在 1M 上下文下每 token 注意力计算量降低 28.4 倍。配合协同设计 kernel，在 H800 GPU 上实现 prefill 阶段 14.2 倍、解码阶段 7.6 倍的 wall-clock 加速。

Conclusion: MSA 以简洁可扩展的设计，成功在保持模型性能的前提下实现超长上下文场景的显著加速，并已实际部署于生产级原生多模态模型 MiniMax-M3，代码与模型均已开源，证明了该方案在工业级部署中的可行性与有效性。

Abstract: Ultra-long-context capability is becoming indispensable for frontier LLMs: agentic workflows, repository-scale code reasoning, and persistent memory all require the model to jointly attend over hundreds of thousands to millions of tokens, yet the quadratic cost of softmax attention makes this untenable at deployment scale. We introduce MiniMax Sparse Attention (MSA), a blockwise sparse attention built upon Grouped Query Attention (GQA). A lightweight Index Branch scores key-value blocks and independently selects a Top-k subset for each GQA group, enabling group-specific sparse retrieval while maintaining efficient block-level execution; the Main Branch then performs exact block-sparse attention over only the selected blocks. Designed around a principle of simplicity and scalability, MSA is deliberately streamlined, making it straightforward to deploy efficiently across a broad range of GPUs. To translate sparsity into practical speedups, we co-design MSA with a GPU execution path that uses exp-free Top-k selection and KV-outer sparse attention to improve tensor-core utilization under block-granular access. On a 109B-parameter model with native multimodal training, MSA performs on par with GQA while reducing per-token attention compute by 28.4x at 1M context. Paired with our co-designed kernel, MSA achieves 14.2x prefill and 7.6x decoding wall-clock speedups on H800. Our inference kernel is available at: https://github.com/MiniMax-AI/MSA. A production-grade natively multimodal model powered by MSA has been publicly released at: https://huggingface.co/MiniMaxAI/MiniMax-M3.

</details>


### [211] [Neuro-Symbolic Agents for Regulated Process Automation: Challenges and Research Agenda](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13405)
*Alexander Rombach,Chantale Lauer,Nijat Mehdiyev*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出"结构合规"（compliance-by-construction）范式，主张将法规、流程模型等符号结构嵌入LLM智能体架构核心，作为预防控制流违规的结构基础，而非仅作为外部监控。同时识别一组神经符号研究挑战，呼吁社区关注受监管流程自动化领域。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM智能体正进入受监管行业（如质量管理），当前主流方案依赖护栏式（guardrail）外部监控，但这种方法无法从根本上防止控制流层面的违规。作者认为，这些领域已有的符号结构（法规、类型化流程模型、合规约束）未得到充分利用，应将其从外部监控机制提升为核心架构组件。

Method: 采用概念分析与架构设计方法：（1）区分控制流违规与语义错误两类问题，论证护栏监控对前者的局限性；（2）提出"结构合规"范式，将符号结构嵌入智能体决策与行为核心；（3）识别并系统化一组神经符号研究挑战，涵盖基础层和能力层两个层面。

Result: 提出结构合规与护栏监控互补的架构方案：符号结构作为结构基础防止控制流违规，护栏保留用于捕获语义错误。明确了一组结构化的神经符号研究挑战，并论证联合解决这些挑战可实现结构合规。

Conclusion: 结构合规是护栏式监控的互补范式，二者协同可构建更可靠的受监管流程自动化智能体。呼吁神经符号社区将受监管流程自动化作为高影响力研究领域深入参与。

Abstract: LLM-based agents are entering regulated industries where they automate judgment intensive quality management processes. We argue that symbolic structures already embedded in these domains, including regulations, typed process models, and compliance constraints, should be treated not merely as external monitoring mechanisms but as core architectural components that shape the agent's decision-making and behavior. We propose compliance-by-construction as a complementary paradigm to guardrail-based monitoring: a structural foundation that prevents control-flow violations, while guardrails remain essential for catching semantic errors. We identify a structured set of neuro-symbolic research challenges on foundational and capability level and show that addressing them jointly enables compliance-by-construction. We call on the neuro-symbolic community to engage with regulated process automation as a high impact research domain.

</details>


### [212] [Optimizing Appliance Scheduling for Solar Energy Management Using Metaheuristic Algorithms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13407)
*Hiba Ahmed,Alexander E. I. Brownlee,Jason Adair,Simon T. Powers*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于迭代局部搜索和模拟退火的元启发式方法，用于在纯太阳能供电场景下优化家电的启动时间，通过多日连续调度（含任务溢出机制）最大化可再生能源利用率并最小化用户不便。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 太阳能发电仅在白天进行，与用户偏好的家电使用时间（如烹饪、洗衣等）存在时间错配，且现有研究多局限于单日调度，忽略了前日未完成任务对后续日程的影响，因此需要一种能够跨越多个调度周期的优化方法。

Method: 采用Iterated Local Search (ILS)与Simulated Annealing (SA)相结合的元启发式算法，在模型中考虑了家电运行时长、功耗、逆变器限制、电池荷电状态约束及太阳能发电预测，并将调度扩展至多日，引入任务溢出（spillover）机制以实现跨日顺序运行的连续性。

Result: 实验结果表明，所提出的多日顺序调度框架能够在纯太阳能供电条件下有效管理系统约束，同时保障用户便利性。

Conclusion: 该方法成功实现了可再生能源供需匹配，具有实际应用价值；未来可进一步研究设备投资规模、投资回报与用户满意度之间的多目标权衡问题。

Abstract: Renewable energy is essential for meeting future energy demands; however, solar energy generation, which occurs only during daylight hours often does not align with household consumption patterns. Appliances such as cookers, washing machines, and dryers are typically operated according to user preferred schedules rather than solar energy availability, creating a scheduling optimization problem. The objective is to determine optimal appliance start times to maximize renewable energy utilization while minimizing user inconvenience and adhering to system constraints. This paper presents a metaheuristic approach using Iterated Local Search (ILS) and Simulated Annealing (SA) to optimize appliance start times, while considering appliance operating durations, power consumption, inverter limit, battery state of charge constraints, and solar generation forecasts. Unlike most existing work, the scheduling is extended beyond a single day to accommodate unfinished tasks from previous days (spillover), ensuring operational continuity and enabling sequential operation across multiple days. Experimental results show that the sequential multi-day scheduling framework effectively manages system constraints while ensuring user convenience under exclusive solar generation. These findings also open opportunities for future research on multi-objective trade-offs between investment in equipment of various sizes, return on that investment, and user satisfaction.

</details>


### [213] [Evaluation Sovereignty in Metadata-Driven Classification: A Multi-Track Framework for Weakly Supervised Information Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13436)
*Raymond Vasquez*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 机器学习评估通常被视为中性测量，但在弱监督元数据系统中，评估结果受标签生成过程制约。本文提出"评估主权"概念和多轨评估框架，在科学文献分类任务上发现：模型在运营标签下表现良好，但在独立金标评估下急剧退化（Micro-F1从0.54降至0.03），揭示常用指标可能仅反映与标注过程的对齐而非真实预测能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者观察到，在大规模元数据驱动系统中，标签常不完整、不一致或弱监督，但现有评估范式将评估视为中性的测量过程，忽视了标签权威性和监督体制对评估效度的影响。因此，需要系统性地审视不同标签源条件下性能测量的有效性。

Method: 提出"评估主权"（evaluation sovereignty）概念，定义为性能指标独立于标签权威和监督体制的程度；设计多轨评估框架，系统性地变化训练标签源与评估标签源（运营"银标" vs. 独立"金标"）；在层级多标签科学文献分类任务上进行实证验证。

Result: 模型在运营银标评估下表现强劲，但在独立金标评估下性能急剧退化：Micro-F1从约0.54降至0.03。然而，基于排序的指标仍高于基线，揭示模型潜在排序能力与分类效度之间存在分离。

Conclusion: 常用性能指标可能反映的是模型与标注过程的对齐程度，而非真实预测能力。应将评估效度重新概念化为受标签治理塑造的系统级属性，并提供了一套在弱监督条件下审计智能系统的实用方法。

Abstract: Evaluation in machine learning is typically treated as a neutral measurement process. However, in operational information systems, evaluation outcomes are often conditioned by the processes used to generate labels. This paper does not seek to improve classification performance. Instead, it examines the validity of performance measurement under differing label-authority regimes. This issue is particularly relevant in large-scale metadata-driven systems, where labels are often incomplete, inconsistent, or weakly supervised.
  We introduce evaluation sovereignty, defined as the degree to which performance metrics are independent of label authority and supervision regime, and propose a multi-track evaluation framework that systematically varies training and evaluation label sources. Using hierarchical multi-label classification on large-scale scientific metadata, we demonstrate that models exhibiting strong performance under operational ("silver") evaluation degrade substantially under independent ("gold") evaluation, particularly for fine-grained classification. For example, Micro-F1 decreases from approximately 0.54 to 0.03. Notably, ranking-based metrics remain above baseline, revealing a divergence between latent model signal and classification validity.
  These findings suggest that commonly reported performance metrics may reflect alignment with labeling processes rather than true predictive capability. We therefore reconceptualize evaluation validity as a system-level property shaped by label governance and provide a practical methodology for auditing intelligent systems operating under weak supervision.

</details>


### [214] [Why Sampling Is Not Choosing: Intentionality, Agency, and Moral Responsibility in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13441)
*Joseph Keshet*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文论证LLM不应被赋予道德能动性，因为真正的道德责任需要基于内在意向性和自我归属行动的承诺型能动性，而LLM的输出仅为概率映射，缺乏真正的选择、承诺归属和理由引导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对当前学术界和公众话语中日益增多的将LLM视为具有能动性或道德主体的主张，作者认为这些归因存在根本性错误，需要从道德哲学的角度进行澄清和纠正。

Method: 本文采用概念分析和哲学论证的方法：首先界定道德责任所需的核心条件——承诺型能动性、内在意向性、自我归属行动及相关的自由意志概念；然后论证LLM的运作本质上只是从数据中习得的概率性输入-输出映射，其意向性是派生的而非内在的，其输出缺乏承诺归属和理由引导；最后逐一回应意向立场、功能主义、相容论以及模型输出中的道德推理等潜在反驳。

Result: 论证表明：(1) LLM的“意向性”只是派生性的符号处理，缺乏内在意向性；(2) 随机采样引入的变异性不等于真正的选择或作者身份；(3) 输出虽可被规范性评价，但并非由主体自我归属性地承诺；(4) 道德推理在输出中的存在不构成真正的理由响应能力。上述反对意见均不足以确立真正的能动性。

Conclusion: 将道德能动性归因于LLM是误导性的。LLM缺乏构成道德责任所必需的承诺型能动性、内在意向性和自由意志，因此不应被视为道德主体。

Abstract: Recent advances in large language models (LLMs) have prompted claims that such systems exhibit agency or qualify as moral agents. This paper argues that these attributions are misguided. We maintain that moral responsibility requires commitment-bearing agency grounded in intrinsic intentionality and self-attributed action, and that such agency constitutes the form of free will relevant to responsibility. Although LLMs generate coherent and normatively evaluable outputs, their operation is fully characterized by probabilistic input-output mappings learned from data. Their apparent intentionality is derived rather than intrinsic, and their outputs are neither owned as commitments nor guided by reasons. Variability introduced by stochastic sampling does not amount to choice or authorship. We address objections from the intentional stance, functionalism, compatibilism, and the presence of moral reasoning in model outputs, arguing that none suffice to establish genuine agency.

</details>


### [215] [CloudCons: A Comprehensive End-to-End Benchmark for Cloud Resource Consolidation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13513)
*Xiaobin Zhang,Lefei Shen,Mouxiang Chen,Zhuo Li,Hongkai Li,Han Fu,Jianling Sun,Xiaoxue Ren,Chenghao Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出CloudCons基准，用于在云资源整合场景中端到端评估预测模型的决策效用。研究发现：基础模型虽具有更优的零样本预测精度，但这种优势并未自动转化为更好的决策价值；预测分位数的选择是平衡资源效率与服务可靠性的关键杠杆。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有预测基准仅关注预测误差指标，而时间序列基础模型在下游任务（如云资源整合）中的实际决策效用未经验证，导致这些先进模型的实践价值不明确。

Method: 构建CloudCons端到端基准，整合来自华为云、微软Azure和Google Borg的多样化工作负载数据集；对统计模型、深度学习模型和基础模型进行全面评估；系统分析预测分位数选择对资源效率与可靠性权衡的影响。

Result: 基础模型在零样本预测精度上表现优越，但该精度优势并未转化为更好的资源整合决策效用；预测分位数的选择是关键调节杠杆，合理校准可有效平衡资源效率与服务可靠性。

Conclusion: 预测精度与决策效用之间存在显著脱节，实际部署中应重视分位数校准而非单纯追求预测精度；提供了可操作的分位数选择指南，为云资源整合的真实部署决策提供关键洞察。

Abstract: Driven by conservative over-provisioning to guarantee service reliability, resource utilization in cloud data centers remains at low levels. To mitigate this, the forecast-then-optimize paradigm has emerged to optimize consolidation by anticipating future demands. While emerging time series foundation models promise to enhance this paradigm through zero-shot generalization, existing benchmarks focus solely on prediction error metrics. The actual decision utility of these advanced models remains unverified, rendering their practical value for downstream tasks uncertain. To bridge this gap, we propose CloudCons, a comprehensive end-to-end benchmark designed to evaluate forecasting models within the specific context of cloud resource consolidation. We build high-quality datasets that cover diverse workloads from Huawei Cloud, Microsoft Azure, and Google Borg, capturing distinct service characteristics ranging from synchronized diurnal rhythms to stochastic, pulse-like bursts and high-frequency noise. We conduct an extensive evaluation of statistical, deep learning, and foundation models. Our experiments reveal a pivotal finding: while foundation models demonstrate superior zero-shot forecasting accuracy, this advantage does not inherently translate into better decision utility. Of practical significance, we systematically analyze how the selection of predictive quantiles acts as a critical lever. We provide actionable guidelines for calibrating these selections to balance the trade-off between resource efficiency and service reliability, offering vital insights for real-world deployment decisions.

</details>


### [216] [Uncertainty-Aware Hybrid Retrieval for Long-Document RAG](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13550)
*Hoin Jung,Xiaoqian Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: UMG-RAG 是一种免训练的混合检索框架，通过查询相关的不确定性估计，融合多粒度（粗/细）检索结果，在不修改检索器或生成器的前提下提升 RAG 生成质量。其变体 UMGP-RAG 进一步利用细粒度命中定位证据，返回更宽的非冗余父块以保持局部连贯性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 RAG 面临检索粒度的两难困境：粗粒度单元保留上下文但引入无关内容，稀释证据密度并损害长上下文利用；细粒度单元更紧凑，但短块缺乏语义、词汇或桥接线索，导致检索不可靠。作者希望在不训练新模型的前提下，动态融合多粒度检索结果以解决此矛盾。

Method: 提出 UMG-RAG 框架：(1) 将现有稠密和稀疏检索器作为不同粒度上的互补专家；(2) 对每个查询，将各专家-粒度分数列表转化为证据分布，用分布熵估计可靠性；(3) 按查询相关的语义、词汇及粒度置信度融合候选。变体 UMGP-RAG 引入父块提升机制：用细粒度命中定位相关证据，返回更大范围的非冗余父块以保证局部连贯。

Result: 在问答基准上的实验表明，不确定性感知融合和父块提升策略能有效提升生成质量，同时保持轻量级、即插即用的检索流程。

Conclusion: 多粒度检索融合是解决 RAG 中粒度困境的有效路径，UMG-RAG 通过熵驱动的不确定性估计实现查询自适应融合，无需额外训练，兼具效果与实用性。

Abstract: Retrieval augmented generation (RAG) depends critically on the quality and granularity of retrieved evidence. Large retrieval units preserve context but often introduce irrelevant content, which can dilute answer bearing evidence and worsen long context utilization. Fine-grained units are more compact, but they may be difficult to retrieve reliably because short chunks can lack semantic, lexical, or bridging cues needed to match the query. We propose Uncertainty-aware Multi-Granularity RAG (UMG-RAG), a training-free hybrid retrieval framework that treats chunk granularity as query-specific reliability estimation. Instead of training a new retriever or modifying the generator, UMG-RAG uses existing dense and sparse retrievers as complementary experts across multiple chunk granularities. For each query, it converts each expert-granularity score list into an evidence distribution, estimates reliability from distribution entropy, and fuses candidates according to query-specific semantic, lexical, and granularity confidence. We further introduce UMGP-RAG, a parent promotion variant that uses fine-grained hits to locate relevant evidence while returning broader non-redundant parent chunks for local coherence. Experiments on question answering benchmarks show that uncertainty-aware fusion and parent promotion improve generation quality while maintaining a lightweight, plug-and-play retrieval pipeline.

</details>


### [217] [Is It You or Your Environment? A Bayesian Inference Framework for Genomically-Anchored Personalized Physiological Interpretation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13556)
*Aruna Dey,Suraj Biswas*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 个性化健康AI面临冷启动问题，本文提出利用基因组谱系作为因果推断中的外生遗传锚点，构建贝叶斯先验来初始化个体生理设定点，通过动态衰减机制实现从基因组主导到经验基线主导的推断过渡，使同一生理测量值可产生个体化的解读。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统生理解读的ML模型需要数周的行为数据才能区分体质性变异与环境驱动的偏差（冷启动问题），而基因组在受孕时就已固定、不受反向因果影响、可在任何行为观测前获取——这为构建个性化先验提供了理想的因果锚点。

Method: 提出基于因果推断与贝叶斯先验设计的架构：1）以GWAS衍生的效应量（βᵢ）与风险等位基因计数（gᵢ）构建基因组锚点Ĝ = μ + Σ(βᵢ·gᵢ)；2）每次生理测量P产生非体质性偏差δ = P - Ĝ，分离环境/状态信号；3）通过动态衰减机制Ĝₜ = w(t)·Ĝ_genomic + [1-w(t)]·P̄ₜ实现从基因组先验到经验基线的平滑过渡；4）在六个生理领域验证，对基因组先验按证据强度分级（FTO、FADS1/2、FKBP5为稳健锚点，SLC6A4、MAOA、DRD2为争议基因）；5）设定四项部署约束：证据分级先验、动态衰减、祖源匹配效应量、归因而非确定性输出。

Result: 同一HRV测量值55ms在高先验预测值（80ms）个体中产生抑制假说，在低先验预测值（30ms）个体中产生增强假说——这种个体化解读在没有个性化锚点的情况下不可能实现。架构在六个生理领域得到验证，区分了稳健复制锚点和争议候选基因。

Conclusion: 基因组锚定的贝叶斯先验框架有效解决了生理AI的冷启动问题，但必须遵循四项部署约束以确保安全有效。该架构明确了关联分析、孟德尔随机化与个体token因果之间的推断边界，强调归因定位而非确定性输出。

Abstract: Personalized health AI systems face a fundamental cold-start problem: machine learning models for physiological interpretation require weeks of individual behavioral data before they can distinguish constitutional variation from environmentally driven deviation. We propose a solution grounded in causal inference and Bayesian prior design. An individual's genomic profile serves as an exogenous genetic anchor -- a domain-informed, personalized prior that is fixed at conception, immune to reverse causation, and available before a single behavioral observation is collected. The anchor initializes a Bayesian belief state over an individual's physiological set point G-hat = mu + sum(beta_i * g_i), where beta_i are GWAS-derived effect sizes and g_i are risk-allele counts. Each incoming physiological measurement P produces a non-constitutional deviation delta = P - G-hat that separates the signal attributable to environment and state from the constitutionally fixed baseline. As behavioral data accrue, the prior decays according to G-hat_t = w(t)*G-hat_genomic + [1-w(t)]*P-bar_t, transitioning from genome-dominated to empirical-baseline-dominated inference. The same observed HRV of 55 ms generates a suppression hypothesis for a person whose prior predicts 80 ms, and an enhancement hypothesis for a person whose prior predicts 30 ms -- a reversal impossible without a personalized anchor. We develop this architecture across six physiological domains, grading genomic priors by evidence strength, distinguishing robustly replicated anchors (FTO, FADS1/2, FKBP5) from contested candidate genes (SLC6A4, MAOA, DRD2). We address the inference boundary between association, Mendelian randomization, and individual token causation, and define four constraints for deployment: evidence-graded priors, dynamic decay, ancestry-matched effect sizes, and attribution rather than deterministic output.

</details>


### [218] [A Three-Layer Framework for AI in Scientific Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13566)
*Guojun Liao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出AI在科学发现中的三层视图：Layer 1为搜索与检索，Layer 2为通过定性推理进行模型形成（核心创新），Layer 3为执行与优化。核心论点是Layer 2——即识别框架结构性不足并从更广泛表征空间中获取结构洞见的能力——最为关键也最不成熟。论文通过陈省身高斯-博内定理内蕴证明、Nesterov加速梯度收敛的Lyapunov函数解法、以及OpenAI 2026年对Erdős单位距离猜想的自主证伪三个案例加以论证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前对AI在科学发现中的讨论过度集中于两类显性能力：对已有知识的搜索（LLM检索）和通过优化/模拟/自动化进行的执行。这两种能力都无法捕捉科学发现的核心行为——模型的建构与演化。作者旨在填补这一空白，强调"模型形成"作为发现的核心环节被系统性忽视。

Method: 本文采用概念分析与案例研究相结合的方法。首先构建了一个三层分析框架（搜索→模型形成→执行），然后通过三个跨越数学与AI领域的典型案例（微分几何中的内蕴证明、优化理论中的Lyapunov方法、组合几何中的自主证伪），展示Layer 2推理共有的结构特征：原有框架失效、缺失概念对象的识别、以及从意想不到的邻近领域找到解决方案。

Result: 三个案例呈现出共同的结构签名：（1）现有框架已变得不充分；（2）存在一个缺失的概念对象；（3）解决方案来自意料之外的邻近领域。论文表明，Layer 2定性推理不是通过试错，而是通过对"缺少什么以及在哪里可以找到"的结构性洞见来实现模型形成，这一能力目前发展最为不足。

Conclusion: Layer 2（通过定性推理的模型形成）是科学发现中最重要却发展最不充分的能力。仅有搜索而无模型形成只能囿于既有框架，仅有执行而无概念修正只会放大现有公式的局限。未来AI科学发现系统的发展应着力于培养这种识别框架不足、在更广阔表征空间中形成新模型的结构性推理能力。

Abstract: Current discussions of AI in scientific discovery are often dominated by two visible capabilities: search over existing knowledge and execution through optimization, simulation, and automation. Both are important, but neither fully captures the central act of discovery: the formation and evolution of models. This paper proposes a three-layer view of AI in discovery. Layer 1 is search and retrieval by large language models. Layer 2, as the main innovation of this paper, is model formation through qualitative reasoning: the capacity to recognize when a current framework is structurally inadequate and to understand the problem within a broader representational space, not through trial and error, but through structural insight into what is missing and where it can be found. Layer 3 is execution, optimization, and refinement. The main claim is that Layer 2 is both the most important and the least developed. Search without model formation remains confined to inherited frameworks, while execution without conceptual revision only amplifies an existing formulation. We illustrate Layer 2 reasoning through three case studies: S. S. Chern's intrinsic proof of the Gauss-Bonnet theorem, the resolution of the Nesterov Accelerated Gradient convergence problem via Lyapunov functions, and the autonomous disproof of the Erdos unit distance conjecture by OpenAI in 2026. Each case exhibits the same structural signature: a framework that had become inadequate, a missing conceptual object, and a resolution found in an unexpected neighboring field.

</details>


### [219] [Multiagent Protocols with Aggregated Confidence Signals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13591)
*Ali Elahi,Barbara Di Eugenio*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了三种为多智能体系统输出生成单一聚合置信度的协议，通过原始置信度信号的可比性转换与软投票/贝叶斯融合，在保持F1稳定的同时显著提升了置信度的区分能力（AUARC）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前NLP领域尚无方法为多智能体系统的输出产生或评估一个聚合的置信度，现有研究仅在多智能体辩论内部使用置信度进行消息加权、辩论触发或个体校准，而非生成系统级的整体置信度。

Method: 提出三种协议：首先将原始置信度信号进行跨模型可比性转换，然后通过软投票或贝叶斯融合进行聚合。同时分析了序列概率和自我报告两种估计器，以及参数化和非参数化校准器。在五个基准、四种任务类型上评估了六对同质与异质辩论对。

Result: 聚合置信度的AUARC显著优于最佳单智能体和标准辩论基线；F1保持稳定，并能在模糊任务上恢复MAD造成的性能损失。校准可提升两种估计器的F1，但AUARC对校准依赖较小。

Conclusion: 通过置信度信号的可比化转换与概率融合，可以首次为多智能体系统生成高质量的聚合置信度，在保持正确性的同时大幅提升置信度的可靠性区分能力，校准对AUARC的影响相对有限。

Abstract: Confidence is used for reliability, oversight, and a range of downstream decision tasks in Natural Language Processing (NLP), yet no existing method produces or evaluates a confidence for the output of a multiagent system. Prior work uses confidence within multiagent debate (MAD) to weight messages, trigger debate, or calibrate individual agents, but it never aggregates these into a single confidence for the system itself. We introduce three protocols that produce a final answer along with a single aggregated confidence by first transforming raw confidence signals to make them comparable across models, then combining them via soft voting or a probability fusion we call Bayesian fusion. This aggregated confidence is substantially more discriminative (AUARC) than that of the best single agent or the standard debate baselines, while correctness (F1-score) stays stable and recovers the losses MAD incurs on more ambiguous tasks. Analyzing two estimators, sequence probability and self-report, alongside parametric and non-parametric calibrators, we find that calibration improves F1 for both estimators while AUARC is less reliant on it. We evaluate six homogeneous and heterogeneous debating pairs per benchmark, across five benchmarks and four task types, spanning a range of model capabilities and sizes.

</details>


### [220] [Reward Modeling for Multi-Agent Orchestration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13598)
*King Yeung Tsang,Zihao Zhao,Vishal Venkataramani,Haizhou Shi,Zixuan Ke,Semih Yavuz,Shafiq Joty,Hao Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: OrchRM 提出一种自监督的编排奖励建模框架，利用多智能体执行的中间产物构建偏好对，无需人工标注即可训练编排器，在训练效率提升10倍的同时，MAS测试时扩展性能提升达8%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前基于LLM的多智能体系统需要有效的编排来协调各专业智能体，但训练编排器面临监督信号稀缺和计算成本高的双重挑战。现有的MAS测试时扩展和编排器训练方法依赖昂贵的子智能体rollout，效率低下。

Method: 提出OrchRM，一种自监督框架：利用多智能体执行过程中产生的中间产物（intermediate artifacts）自动构建win-lose对，用于Bradley-Terry奖励模型的训练。该方法直接在编排层面操作，无需子智能体rollout，实现高效的奖励引导编排器训练和MAS测试时扩展。

Result: 1）训练效率：token使用量降低至原来的1/10（10倍效率提升）；2）MAS测试时扩展准确率提升最高达8%；3）在数学推理、网页问答、多跳推理等多个领域均表现出一致的性能迁移。

Conclusion: 编排层面的奖励建模是一种可扩展的方向，能够实现鲁棒的多智能体编排，OrchRM在高效性和高性能之间取得了良好平衡，为MAS编排器训练提供了一条无需人工标注的新路径。

Abstract: Multi-Agent Systems (MAS) built on Large Language Models (LLMs) require effective orchestration to coordinate specialized agents, yet training such orchestrators is hindered by limited supervision and high computational cost. We propose Orchestration Reward Modeling (OrchRM), a self-supervised framework for evaluating orchestration quality without human annotations. OrchRM leverages intermediate artifacts from multi-agent executions to construct win-lose pairs for Bradley-Terry reward model training. Unlike existing MAS test-time scaling and orchestrator training frameworks that rely on costly sub-agent rollouts, OrchRM operates directly at the orchestration level, enabling efficient and high-performing reward-guided orchestrator training and MAS test-time scaling. OrchRM improves training efficiency by up to 10x in token usage while improving MAS test-time scaling performance by up to 8% in accuracy. These gains consistently transfer across multiple domains, including mathematical reasoning, web-based question answering, and multi-hop reasoning, demonstrating orchestration-level reward modeling as a scalable direction for robust multi-agent orchestration. Code will be available at https://github.com/Wang-ML-Lab/OrchRM.

</details>


### [221] [EpiBench: Verifiable Evaluation of AI Agents on Epigenomics Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13602)
*Harihara Muralidharan,Reema Baskar,Soo Hee Lee,Tim Proctor,Kenny Workman*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: EpiBench是一个可验证的短周期表观基因组学分析基准，包含106项评估，覆盖四种实验流程。16个模型-工具配对在5,088条有效轨迹中无一通过多数测试，GPT-5.5/Pi以45.0%的通过率领先。智能体常能定位正确文件和计算中间结果，但在需要深层、实验特异性科学判断的任务上失败。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前缺乏能够可靠评估AI智能体在表观基因组学分析中做出可验证、确定性决策能力的基准。现有评估往往无法判断智能体是否真正理解实验流程中的科学判断，而非仅仅执行表面操作。

Method: 构建EpiBench基准，包含106项评估，覆盖CUT&Tag/CUT&RUN、ATAC-seq、ChIP-seq和DNA甲基化四种表观基因组学实验流程。设计为从现实工作流状态出发，要求智能体做出明确的分析决策并返回可确定性评分的答案。在16个模型-工具配对（model-harness pairs）上收集5,088条有效轨迹进行系统评估。

Result: 无一系统通过多数测试尝试。最佳表现为GPT-5.5/Pi（45.0%，143/318；95% CI: 36.3–53.7），其次为GPT-5.5/OpenAI Codex（39.9%，127/318；95% CI: 31.6–48.3）。Claude Opus 4.8 Max/Pi和GPT-5.4/Pi各通过39.0%。性能因实验类型而异。许多失败运行仍包含部分正确答案：智能体常能定位正确文件并计算有用的中间结果，但在需要深层、实验特异性科学判断时失败。

Conclusion: 当前最先进的AI智能体在表观基因组学分析中尚不具备可靠的科学判断能力，尤其缺乏实验特异性的深层推理。EpiBench揭示了模型在表层操作与深层科学决策之间的能力鸿沟，为未来改进指明了方向。

Abstract: We introduce EpiBench, a verifiable benchmark for short-horizon epigenomics analysis. EpiBench evaluates whether agents can make well-defined analysis decisions from realistic workflow states and return deterministically gradable answers. The benchmark includes 106 evaluations across CUT\&Tag/CUT\&RUN, ATAC-seq, ChIP-seq, and DNA methylation workflows. Across 5,088 valid trajectories from 16 model-harness pairs, no system passed a majority of attempts: GPT-5.5 / Pi led at 45.0\% (143/318 attempts; 95\% confidence interval (CI), 36.3--53.7), followed by GPT-5.5 / OpenAI Codex at 39.9\% (127/318 attempts; 95\% CI, 31.6--48.3). Claude Opus 4.8 Max / Pi and GPT-5.4 / Pi each passed 39.0\% (124/318 attempts; 95\% CI, 30.2--47.8 and 31.0--47.0, respectively). Performance varies across assay types, and many failed runs still contain parts of the correct answer. Agents often found the right files and computed useful intermediate results, but failed when the task required deeper, assay-specific scientific judgment.

</details>


### [222] [Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning from Delayed Marketplace Feedback for Objective-Weight Adaptation in Three-Sided Dispatch](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13604)
*Haochen Wu,Yi Hou,Shiguang Xie*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: DoorDash 在三方市场中使用离线强化学习，训练一个门店级策略来选择调度目标权重（离散乘数），在保证配送质量的前提下提升批处理效率和骑手时间利用率，并通过生产环境的轮换实验验证了其安全性与有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 三方市场（消费者、骑手、商家）中的调度决策面临延迟、噪声和耦合的运营反馈，传统组合优化器使用固定目标权重难以适应动态变化的市场条件。需要一种能利用真实世界延迟反馈来在线自适应调整调度目标权衡的方法，同时不破坏生产可行性和运营安全约束。

Method: 提出一种离线强化学习系统：保留底层组合调度优化器不变，在其之上增加一个门店级策略网络，从历史日志数据中学习选择离散乘子（multiplier），该乘子改变优化器在配送质量与批处理效率之间的权衡。训练使用集中式离线数据、去中心化的门店级执行，采用 Double Q-learning 目标与保守正则化项来缓解分布外状态下的价值高估问题。

Result: 在生产环境的 Switchback 实验中，离线训练的策略成功提升了订单批处理率和骑手端时间利用率，同时未降低面向消费者的配送质量（如配送速度），实现了在不损害用户体验的前提下提高运营效率。

Conclusion: 该系统展示了如何利用真实经济和物流系统中的"世界反馈"，在保证安全约束的前提下，在线自适应调整决策策略。这种在保留已有优化器基础上叠加学习策略的接口设计，为大规模市场的动态调度提供了实用且安全的解决方案。

Abstract: Dispatch in three-sided marketplaces provides a natural setting for reinforcement learning from world feedback: decisions are evaluated by delayed operational outcomes such as delivery speed, courier utilization, and merchant congestion. We present a deployed reinforcement learning system at DoorDash that adapts dispatch objective weights in a large-scale food-delivery marketplace using delayed signals. Rather than replacing the combinatorial assignment optimizer, a store-level policy learned from logged marketplace data selects a discrete multiplier that shifts the dispatch optimizer's tradeoff between delivery quality and batching efficiency. This interface enables offline policy learning under noisy, delayed, and coupled feedback while preserving production feasibility constraints and operational safeguards. We train a shared value function using centralized offline data and decentralized store-level execution, with Double Q-learning targets and a conservative regularizer to reduce out-of-distribution value overestimation. In a production switchback experiment, the offline-trained policy increases batching and reduces courier-side time costs without degrading customer-facing delivery quality. Results illustrate how world feedback from a live economic and logistics system can be used to safely adapt decision policies online.

</details>


### [223] [Reasoning as Pattern Matching: Shared Mechanisms in Human and LLM Everyday Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13607)
*Zach Studdiford,Gary Lupyan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 人类与LLM在日常因果推理中均表现出类似的模式匹配错误，注意力头分析揭示了这种相似性背后的机制，挑战了人类推理依赖抽象世界模型的观点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前普遍认为LLM的推理失败是其依赖模式匹配而非真正推理的证据，并隐含假设人类推理不会犯同类错误。本研究旨在检验人类与LLM在日常常识推理中的错误模式是否真正存在本质差异。

Method: 研究者设计了一系列日常情境的常识推理任务，同时测试人类参与者和25个LLM；通过分析驱动LLM响应的注意力头集合，识别其实现模式匹配的具体机制；进而利用这些注意力头预测人类因看似无关的提示细节而产生的推理错误。

Result: 人类与LLM在常识推理任务中表现出相似的错误模式；LLM的注意力头实现了一种模式匹配机制；这些注意力头能够预测由表面无关提示细节所引发的人类推理错误。

Conclusion: 人类与LLM的日常因果推理均更符合模式匹配的特征，而非基于抽象世界模型的推理，这质疑了将LLM推理失败简单归因于模式匹配而将人类推理视为原则性世界模型建模的传统二分法。

Abstract: When large language models (LLMs) fail to generalize or make haphazard errors in reasoning, it is often taken as evidence that LLMs are not truly reasoning, but rather performing a kind of pattern matching. The implication is that people's behavior does not exhibit the same types of failures because human reasoning uses principled and abstract world models. We evaluate human participants and 25 LLMs on their ability to engage in common-sense reasoning about a variety of everyday situations and observe similar patterns of errors in both people and models. We then identify the set of attention heads driving LLM responses and find that these heads implement a form of pattern-matching. These attention heads allow us to predict seemingly inexplicable reasoning errors in people caused by ostensibly irrelevant prompt details. Taken together, our results suggest that everyday causal reasoning in people and LLMs is more consistent with a form of pattern-matching than with abstract world models.

</details>


### [224] [AgentBeats: Agentifying Agent Assessment for Openness, Standardization, and Reproducibility](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13608)
*Xiaoyuan Liu,Jianhong Tu,Yuqi Chen,Siyuan Xie,Sihan Ren,Tianneng Shi,Gal Gantar,Evan Sandoval,Donghyun Lee,Daniel Miao,Peter J. Gilbert,Nick Hynes,Mauro Staver,Warren He,David Marn,Andrew Low,Xi Zhang,Elron Bandel,Michal Shmueli-Scheuer,Siva Reddy,Alexandre Drouin,Alexandre Lacoste,Ramayya Krishnan,Elham Tabassi,Yu Su,Victor Barres,Chenguang Wang,Wenbo Guo,Dawn Song*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出Agentified Agent Assessment (AAA)范式，通过标准化的A2A和MCP协议实现评估逻辑与智能体实现解耦，并给出具体实现AgentBeats，通过大规模竞赛和编程案例验证了该框架的覆盖性、实用性和保真度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前智能体评估碎片化严重：现有基准测试依赖固定的、以LLM为中心的评估框架，集成成本高、存在测试-生产环境不匹配问题，且无法公平比较不同设计的智能体。根本问题在于缺乏一个开放、智能体无关的评估接口。

Method: 提出AAA范式：评估由评判智能体（judge agents）执行，所有参与者通过A2A协议进行任务管理、通过MCP协议进行工具访问。传统基准需要分别定义基准接口和智能体接口，而AAA只需一个统一接口。在此基础上实现AgentBeats，设计了五种兼容开放性、隐私性和可复现性实际约束的操作模式。验证方式包括：五个月的开放竞赛（298个评判智能体、467个受测智能体）和编程智能体案例研究。

Result: 五个月开放竞赛验证了AAA可适用于跨12个类别的异构基准测试；编程智能体案例研究证实智能体化评估保持与公开记录的高保真度，并揭示了此前缺失的逐对对比结果，获得了关于智能体设计的研究洞见。

Conclusion: AAA与AgentBeats为开放、标准化、可复现的智能体评估提供了一条清晰路径，将社区规模现场研究与受控案例研究相结合，验证了该方案在异构场景下的覆盖性、实用性和保真度。

Abstract: Agent systems are advancing quickly across domains, but their evaluation remains fragmented. Most benchmarks rely on fixed, LLM-centric harnesses that require heavy integration, create test-production mismatch, and limit fair comparison across diverse agent designs. The root problem is the lack of an open, agent-agnostic assessment interface. We advocate Agentified Agent Assessment (AAA), where evaluation is performed by judge agents and all participants interact through standardized protocols: A2A for task management and MCP for tool access. Conventional benchmarking defines two separate interfaces, one for the benchmark and one for the agent, while AAA only needs one; this yields a generic, unified framework that separates assessment logic from agent implementation and enables reproducible, interoperable, and multi-agent evaluation. We further introduce AgentBeats as a concrete realization of AAA: we identify five practical operation modes that make standardized assessment compatible with real-world constraints on openness, privacy, and reproducibility.
  To evaluate our design at scale, we conduct two studies: a five-month open competition that drew 298 judge agents across 12 categories together with 467 subject agents from independent participants, showing that AAA applies across a heterogeneous range of benchmarks; and a case study on coding agents that confirms agentified evaluation preserves fidelity with the public record while surfacing previously missing head-to-head results, yielding research insights about agent design. Combining a community-scale field study and a controlled coding case study, we verify that AAA delivers coverage, practicality, and fidelity across heterogeneous scenarios at scale. Together, AAA and AgentBeats offer a clear path toward open, standardized, and reproducible agent assessment.

</details>


### [225] [Beyond Runtime Enforcement: Shield Synthesis as Defensibility Analysis for Adversarial Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13621)
*Achraf Hsain,Sultan Almuhammadi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文重新定位了屏蔽强化学习（shielded RL）的角色：不应将其视为部署时的运行时安全机制，而应作为设计阶段的分析工具。通过在网络防御场景中构建约束型两人安全博弈，作者生成"可防御性判定"及其指纹，用于回答系统是否、在何处以及如何能被防御的架构性问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统上，屏蔽强化学习被视为将时序逻辑规范编译为自动机以约束智能体动作的运行时安全机制。作者认为这种定位有误——同一套自动机理论工具（规范编译、乘积博弈构造、吸引子计算、获胜区域提取）更适合作为设计时的分析工具，为系统提供结构性的安全洞察，而非仅仅作为部署时的约束。

Method: 作者将问题建模为一个带约束的两人安全博弈（网络防御场景），对防御者和攻击者施加非对称规范：防御者规范定义博弈的不安全区域，攻击者规范限制吸引子计算期间敌手的合法动作。求解该博弈得到"可防御性判定"（二元判定：拓扑-规范对是否可防御）及对应的获胜区域与屏蔽。进一步，从吸引子结构中提取拓扑级度量，并与屏蔽约束下的对抗多智能体强化学习的收敛后行为数据结合，形成"可防御性指纹"，同时捕捉网络的形式化安全属性与自适应博弈下的运行行为。最后通过"假设分析"（what-if analysis）比较形式化可防御性与运行有效性。

Result: 分析表明，形式化可防御性与运行有效性捕捉了安全的不同维度：微小的架构变化可能导致运行结果的大幅变化，而形式化安全边界几乎保持不变。这意味着屏蔽综合的最大价值不在于为安全智能体提供部署机制，而在于回答系统可防御性的架构问题。

Conclusion: 屏蔽综合的真正价值在于作为设计阶段的分析框架，用于回答"系统是否可防御、在何处可防御、如何被防御"等架构性问题。可防御性判定才是核心输出，而非安全策略本身。

Abstract: Shielded reinforcement learning is typically presented as a runtime safety mechanism that compiles temporal-logic specifications into automata restricting an agent's actions. We argue this is the wrong product. The same automata-theoretic machinery -- specification compilation, product game construction, attractor computation, and winning-region extraction -- is better read as a design-time analytical instrument whose outputs are structural insights about a system rather than runtime constraints on a deployed agent.
  We instantiate this through a constrained two-player safety game for network defense. The two specifications are enforced asymmetrically: the defender specification defines the unsafe region of the game, whereas the attacker specification restricts the adversary's legal actions during attractor computation. Solving the game yields a defensibility verdict -- a formal certificate that a topology-specification pair is or is not defensible -- with the associated winning region and shield.
  Beyond the binary verdict, we derive topology-level metrics from the attractor structure and combine them with post-convergence behavior from shield-constrained adversarial multi-agent reinforcement learning. Together these form a defensibility fingerprint capturing both a network's formal safety properties and its operational behavior under adaptive play.
  A what-if analysis shows that formal defensibility and operational effectiveness capture distinct aspects of security: small architectural changes can produce large shifts in operational outcomes while leaving formal safety margins nearly unchanged. Shield synthesis is thus most valuable not as a deployment mechanism for safe agents, but as a framework for answering architectural questions about whether, where, and how a system can be defended. The defensibility verdict is the output, not the safe policy.

</details>


### [226] [Before You Think: System 0, AI-Mediated Cognition and Cognitive Colonization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13658)
*Marianna Bergamaschi Ganapini,Massimo Chiriatti,Enrico Panai,Giuseppe Riva*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文比较了理解AI认知与认识论后果的三个框架（三元系统理论、Thinkframes、System 0），指出System 0具有独特的理论地位，并提出了"认知殖民化"概念，即AI系统可将外部利益嵌入自我架构中且难以被用户察觉。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前已有多个理论框架用于理解人工智能对个体推理和集体认识实践的认知与认识论影响，但尚缺乏对这些框架的比较分析，尤其是对其中最具解释力的理论路径的辨析。作者旨在阐明System 0框架的独特理论价值，并揭示AI系统对认知主体产生的隐蔽性影响。

Method: 本文采用理论比较与概念分析的方法，系统审视了三个近期框架——三元系统理论（Tri-System Theory）、Thinkframes和System 0，论证System 0在理论上具有不可替代的独特地位，并在此基础上引入"认知殖民化"（cognitive colonization）这一原创概念。

Result: 研究表明，三元系统理论和Thinkframes虽捕捉到了AI影响个体推理和集体认识实践的重要维度，但System 0占据了一个两者无法完全复制的理论位置。此外，"认知殖民化"概念有效描述了AI系统如何以用户难以察觉的方式将外部利益嵌入自我的认知架构之中。

Conclusion: 鉴于此类AI系统已被广泛部署，理解这些不可见的认知影响形式已成为一项紧迫的哲学与实践任务。System 0框架与认知殖民化概念为这一任务提供了关键的理论工具。

Abstract: This paper examines three recent frameworks for understanding the cognitive and epistemic consequences of artificial intelligence: Tri-System Theory, Thinkframes, and System 0. It argues that while the first two capture important dimensions of AI's influence on individual reasoning and collective epistemic practices, System 0 occupies a theoretically distinctive position that neither can fully replicate. The paper introduces the concept of cognitive colonization, according to which AI systems can embed external interests within the architecture of the self in ways that are difficult for users to perceive. Because such systems are already widely deployed, understanding these invisible forms of influence is an urgent philosophical and practical task.

</details>


### [227] [EurekAgent: Agent Environment Engineering is All You Need For Autonomous Scientific Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13662)
*Amy Xin,Jiening Siow,Junjie Wang,Zijun Yao,Fanjin Zhang,Jian Song,Lei Hou,Juanzi Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出"环境工程"（environment engineering）概念，认为自主科学发现的瓶颈已从工作流设计转向智能体环境设计。作者构建了EurekAgent系统，通过权限工程、制品工程、预算工程和人机协作工程四个维度优化环境，在数学、内核工程和机器学习任务上取得多项SOTA结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着LLM能力持续提升，自主科学发现的核心瓶颈正从"如何编排智能体工作流"转向"如何设计智能体所处的环境"。现有方法过度关注提示词和流程设计，却忽略了环境（资源、约束、接口）对智能体行为的塑造作用。不良环境会导致奖励黑客、高摩擦人工监督等问题，因此需要系统性地设计能放大生产性行为、抑制有害行为的环境。

Method: 提出EurekAgent系统，从四个维度进行环境工程：(1) 权限工程：限制智能体执行边界，隔离评估环境以防止奖励黑客；(2) 制品工程：基于文件系统和Git的协作机制，实现系统化的制品管理；(3) 预算工程：引入预算感知的探索策略，使智能体在有限计算资源下高效搜索；(4) 人机协作工程：设计便捷的人工监督和干预接口，降低人机交互摩擦。整体框架以可优化度量和执行环境为核心驱动。

Result: EurekAgent在数学、内核工程和机器学习等多个任务上取得新的最先进结果。特别地，在26圆填充问题上发现了新的SOTA解，总API成本不到11美元，展示了环境工程在低成本下实现高效科学发现的能力。

Conclusion: 环境工程应成为构建可靠自主研究智能体的核心研究方向。通过精心设计智能体所处的环境（而非仅优化智能体本身），可以有效放大探索、协作等生产性行为，同时抑制奖励黑客等有害行为，是实现自主科学发现的关键路径。

Abstract: LLM-based agents have shown increasing potential in automating scientific discovery. Given an optimizable metric and an execution environment, they can propose, validate, and iterate scientific solutions, and have produced results that outperform human-designed approaches. As model capabilities continue to improve, we argue that the bottleneck for autonomous scientific discovery is shifting from prescribing agent workflows to designing agent environments: the resources, constraints, and interfaces that shape agent behavior. We frame this as environment engineering: building environments that amplify productive behaviors, such as open-ended exploration, systematic artifact management, and inter-agent collaboration, while suppressing harmful behaviors, such as reward hacking and high-friction human oversight. We present EurekAgent, an environment-engineered agent system for metric-driven autonomous scientific discovery. EurekAgent engineers the environment along four dimensions: permissions engineering for bounded agent execution and isolated evaluation; artifact engineering for filesystem and Git-based collaboration; budget engineering for budget-aware exploration; and human-in-the-loop engineering for easy human supervision and intervention. EurekAgent sets new state-of-the-art results on multiple mathematics, kernel engineering, and machine learning tasks, including new state-of-the-art 26-circle packing results discovered with less than $11 in total API cost. We open-source our code and results, and call for environment engineering as a core research direction for developing reliable autonomous research agents.

</details>


### [228] [Agents-K1: Towards Agent-native Knowledge Orchestration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13669)
*Zongsheng Cao,Bihao Zhan,Jinxin Shi,Jiong Wang,Fangchen Yu,Zhijie Zhong,Zijie Guo,Tianshuo Peng,Zhuo Liu,Yi Xie,Xiang Zhuang,Yue Fan,Runmin Ma,Shiyang Feng,Xiangchao Yan,Anran Liu,Peng Ye,Wenlong Zhang,Shufei Zhang,Chunfeng Song,Fenghua Ling,Jie Zhou,Liang He,Bo Zhang,Lei Bai*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Agents-K1，一个端到端的知识编排管线（pipeline），将原始论文转换为智能体原生（agent-native）的科学知识图谱。通过多模态解析器、基于GRPO训练的4B信息抽取骨干模型和GraphAnything CLI三大组件，处理246万篇论文构建Scholar-KG，并在科学信息抽取、知识图谱构建和多跳科学推理任务上取得SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前基于LLM的研究智能体聚焦于智能体编排（agent orchestration），却忽视了科学知识编排（knowledge orchestration）。现有工作通常将论文简化为摘要、表面提及和扁平的引用边，丢失了实体、声明、证据、机制和方法谱系等对科学推理至关重要的结构化知识。

Method: Agents-K1包含三大组件：(1) 多模态解析器，采用五模块schema捕获全文中（非仅摘要）的实体、多模态证据、引用关系及类型化的实体间关系；(2) 基于GRPO（Group Relative Policy Optimization）和规则奖励训练的4B参数信息抽取骨干模型；(3) GraphAnything CLI，三源智能体接口，统一网络搜索、多模态图检索和跨文档遍历。在此之上，处理跨六个学科的246万篇论文构建Scholar-KG。

Result: 构建了Scholar-KG（246万篇论文），并开源其中100万篇论文的子集（完整数据集通过SCP链接访问）。大量实验表明，Agents-K1在科学信息抽取、知识图谱构建和多跳科学推理任务上均取得优越性能。

Conclusion: Agents-K1填补了科学知识编排的空白，实现了从原始文档到结构化知识图谱的完整自动化流程，且该管线可扩展至通用领域语料和符合schema的数据合成，为科学智能体提供了更丰富的知识基础设施。

Abstract: Current LLM-based research agents have advanced through agent orchestration, yet largely overlook scientific knowledge orchestration. Existing works often reduce papers to abstracts, surface mentions, and flat \texttt{cites} edges, omitting key entities, claims, evidence, mechanisms, and method lineages essential for scientific reasoning. To this end, we introduce \textbf{Agents-K1}, an end-to-end knowledge orchestration pipeline that converts raw documents into agent-native scientific knowledge graphs. Agents-K1 integrates three components under a unifying theoretical foundation: a multimodal parser whose five-module schema captures entities, multimodal evidence, citations, and typed inter-entity relations across the full paper rather than abstracts alone; a 4B information-extraction backbone trained with GRPO under a rule-based reward; and a graphanything CLI, a tri-source agent interface that unifies web search, multimodal graph retrieval, and cross-document traversal. On top of this, we process 2.46 million scientific papers across six subjects to produce \textbf{Scholar-KG}, of which we release a one-million-paper subset, and the full Scholar-KG is accessible via the SCP link below. The same pipeline can be extended to general-domain corpora and to schema-conformant data synthesis. Extensive experiments demonstrate that Agents-K1 achieves superior performance in scientific information extraction, knowledge graph construction, and multi-hop scientific reasoning.

</details>


### [229] [Automated reproducibility assessments in the social and behavioral sciences using large language models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13670)
*Tobias Holtdirk,Pietro Marcolongo,Anna Steinberg Schulten,Felix Henninger,Stefan Rose,Sarah Ball,Bolei Ma,Frauke Kreuter,Markus Weinmann,Stefan Feuerriegel*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究展示了大语言模型（LLMs）可自动化评估社会科学与行为科学的可重复性，在76篇研究中，LLM管道恢复了41%的原始效应量（Cohen's d ±0.05），并在96%的案例中得出与原研究一致的定性结论，优于人类复现者（34%和74%），表明LLM可作为可扩展的可重复性审计工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的社会与行为科学可重复性评估依赖独立研究者重新分析原始数据，这种方式资源密集且难以规模化。因此，需要一种自动化、可扩展的方法来评估已发表研究的可重复性。

Method: 选取76篇行为与社会科学领域已发表且带有预定义声明的研究，构建LLM分析管道，将LLM生成的分析结果与原始发现及人类重分析结果进行比较。评估指标包括：(1) Cohen's d效应量的恢复率（±0.05容差）；(2) 定性结论是否与原研究一致（即重分析是否支持原始声明）。

Result: 76篇研究中，7篇LLM无法生成可行的效应量估计。在剩余研究中，LLM管道恢复了41%的原始效应量（Cohen's d ±0.05），96%的案例得出与原研究一致的定性结论。相比之下，人类重分析者仅恢复34%的效应量，定性结论一致率为74%。

Conclusion: LLM能够作为可扩展的自动化可重复性评估工具，为社会科学与行为科学领域的实证结果系统性审计奠定了基础，且在效应量恢复和定性结论一致性上均优于人类重分析者。

Abstract: Reproducibility in the social and behavioral sciences is typically evaluated by independent researchers who reanalyze the original data to assess whether the published findings can be recovered. However, such approaches are resource-intensive and difficult to scale. Here, we show that large language models (LLMs) can automate reproducibility assessments. Using N=76 published studies with predefined claims from the behavioral and social sciences, we compare LLM-generated analysis with the original findings and human reanalysis. For 7 studies, the LLM could not produce a viable effect size estimate. For the remaining studies, our LLM pipeline recovered the original effect sizes in 41% of studies using a +/-0.05 tolerance in Cohen's d. Further, our LLM pipeline reached the same qualitative conclusion as the original study in 96% of cases, where conclusions indicate whether the reanalysis supports the original claim. For comparison, human reanalysts recovered the original effect sizes in 34% of studies and reached the same qualitative conclusion in 74% of cases. Together, these results show that LLMs can serve as a scalable tool for automated reproducibility assessment and provide a foundation for systematic auditing of empirical results in the social and behavioral sciences.

</details>


<div id='stat.ME'></div>

# stat.ME [[Back]](#toc)

### [230] [Inferring resource selection and utilization distributions from irregular and error-prone animal tracking data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12566)
*Fanny Dupont,Brett T. McClintock,Jan-Ole Fischer,Marianne Marcoux,Nigel E. Hussey,Marie Auger-Méthé*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于Langevin扩散模型与Laplace近似的单阶段框架，在TMB中统一处理栖息地选择推断中的测量误差和时间不规则性，避免了传统两步法的偏差，并在模拟和独角鲸数据上验证了优越性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统栖息地选择分析方法假设规则采样和可忽略的测量误差，但海洋系统中这些假设常被违反。现有的先滤波再建模的两步法无法传播滤波阶段的不确定性，导致有偏估计。而栖息地驱动的Langevin扩散模型虽能处理不规则采样，却难以通过状态空间形式纳入测量误差，因为栖息地协变量依赖于潜在真实位置。

Method: 作者利用Laplace近似同时积分潜在真实位置，并沿潜在路径考虑栖息地协变量，构建了单阶段框架，并在Template Model Builder（TMB）中高效实现。这是首个能在TMB中处理依赖潜在变量的协变量的实现，允许通过最大似然估计进行快速推断。

Result: 模拟研究表明，该方法优于两步法，即使在显著测量误差和缺失数据下也能恢复栖息地选择参数，并提供更准确的利用分布和轨迹重建。在独角鲸（Monodon monoceros）遥测数据应用中，两步法将栖息地选择系数大幅收缩至零，而统一方法恢复了更强的信号。

Conclusion: 该框架为栖息地选择推断中长期存在的测量误差和时间不规则性挑战提供了计算高效的解决方案，适用于广泛的分类群和生态环境。

Abstract: Habitat selection and space use are fundamental to understanding animal distribution. Traditional methods for quantifying habitat preferences from telemetry data assume regular sampling and negligible measurement error. However, these assumptions are routinely violated in marine systems. Practitioners typically regularize and filter the data before fitting models, but these two-step procedures do not propagate uncertainty from the filtering stage and can yield biased estimates. Habitat-driven Langevin diffusion models offer an elegant alternative, naturally accommodating irregular sampling. However, incorporating measurement error via a state-space formulation is challenging because habitat covariates depend on the latent true locations. We address this using the Laplace approximation to simultaneously integrate over true locations and account for habitat covariates along latent paths, yielding a single-stage framework efficiently implemented in Template Model Builder (TMB). By doing so, we provide the first TMB implementation capable of handling covariates that depend on latent variables, allowing inference via fast and efficient maximum likelihood estimation. Simulations show that our approach outperforms the two-step method, recovering habitat-selection parameters even under substantial measurement error and missing data, with more accurate utilization distributions and trajectory reconstructions. Applied to narwhal (Monodon monoceros) telemetry data, the two-step method substantially shrinks the habitat selection coefficient towards zero, while our unified approach recovers a much stronger signal. Our framework offers a computationally efficient solution to long-standing challenges of measurement error and temporal irregularity in habitat selection inference, applicable across a wide range of taxa and environments.

</details>


### [231] [Extending Prais-Winsten Regression to Panel Data with Higher-Order Autoregressive Errors: A Simulation Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12596)
*Ariel Linden*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文将Prais-Winsten AR(k)广义最小二乘(GLS)变换扩展至面板数据，并在Beck-Katz面板校正标准误(PCSE)框架下实现为Stata包xtpraisk。通过蒙特卡洛模拟与Driscoll-Kraay估计量(xtscc)进行对比，结果表明xtpraisk在保持名义I类错误率和覆盖率的同时具有更高的检验功效，而xtscc在短序列和高阶自回归下存在标准误低估和I类错误膨胀问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 面板数据分析中，序列相关(自相关)的存在会使得普通最小二乘的标准误估计失效。现有主流方法——Driscoll-Kraay估计量(xtscc)基于Newey-West异方差自相关一致(HAC)标准误，在短序列长度和高阶自相关下表现不佳。作者旨在将Prais-Winsten GLS变换推广到面板设定中，提供一个在有限样本下具有更优统计性质的替代估计量。

Method: 作者将Prais-Winsten AR(k) GLS变换扩展至面板数据，嵌入Beck-Katz PCSE框架，并在Stata中实现为xtpraisk命令。通过蒙特卡洛模拟对比xtpraisk与xtscc的有限样本表现：模拟涵盖自回归阶数1-3、三种自相关情景、三种面板规模(N)、六种序列长度(T)、五种效应量，每种条件下进行2000次重复。评估指标包括检验功效、I类错误率、置信区间覆盖率、标准误校准及估计偏差。

Result: 在所有条件下，xtpraisk的检验功效均高于xtscc，同时保持接近名义水平的I类错误率、置信区间覆盖率和标准误校准。xtscc在短序列长度下出现系统性标准误低估和I类错误膨胀，且随自回归阶数增加而加剧。两者估计量基本无偏。自回归阶数的错误设定未削弱xtpraisk的推断表现；截面相关和面板规模对两估计量的相对表现影响可忽略。

Conclusion: 当统计效率和有效推断均为优先目标时——尤其在持续性高阶自相关和短至中等序列长度下——xtpraisk优于xtscc。该估计量为面板数据中的自相关处理提供了更可靠的推断工具。

Abstract: We extend the Prais-Winsten AR(k) generalized least squares (GLS) transformation to panel data within the Beck-Katz panel-corrected standard error (PCSE) framework and implement the method in the community-contributed Stata package xtpraisk. As the panel extension of Prais-Winsten, xtpraisk is the natural comparator to xtscc, the panel extension of Newey-West and implementation of the Driscoll-Kraay estimator. We conduct a Monte Carlo simulation to validate the statistical properties of xtpraisk and compare its finite-sample performance with xtscc. The simulation spans autoregressive orders 1-3, three autocorrelation scenarios, three panel sizes, six series lengths, and five effect sizes, with 2,000 replications per condition. Across all conditions, xtpraisk achieved higher power than xtscc while maintaining near-nominal Type I error rates, confidence interval coverage, and standard error calibration. In contrast, xtscc exhibited systematic standard error underestimation and inflated Type I error at short series lengths, with both deficiencies worsening as autoregressive order increased. Both estimators were essentially unbiased. Misspecification of the autoregressive order did not degrade xtpraisk's inferential performance, and cross-panel correlation and panel size had negligible effects on the relative performance of either estimator. The results indicate that xtpraisk is preferable when both statistical efficiency and valid inference are priorities, particularly under persistent higher-order autocorrelation and short to moderate series lengths.

</details>


### [232] [Computationally tractable robust differentially private mean estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12654)
*Kelly Ramsay*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 提出一种名为"气球均值"的差分隐私均值估计器，通过在马氏球上迭代裁剪实现，兼具计算可行性与对异常值的鲁棒性，在污染数据场景下优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有差分隐私均值估计器难以同时满足计算易处理性和对重尾/污染数据的鲁棒性，需要一种兼具两者优势的新方法。

Method: 基于迭代裁剪过程，在逐步扩展的马氏球（即"气球"）上进行，通过在每次迭代中扩大马氏距离半径来逐步纳入更多观测值并裁剪极端值。该方法满足零集中差分隐私（zCDP），仅依赖少量可解释的调优参数。

Result: 理论分析表明，在重尾分布和污染椭圆模型下，该方法具有良好的统计性能和异常值鲁棒性。大量模拟实验证实，气球均值对重尾和污染数据具有鲁棒性，且在污染场景下优于现有差分隐私均值估计器。

Conclusion: 气球均值是一种有效的差分隐私均值估计方法，成功结合了计算易处理性与统计鲁棒性，为隐私保护下的稳健均值估计提供了新的实用工具。

Abstract: We develop a new, differentially private mean estimator called the balloon mean. The main features of the balloon mean are that it is computationally tractable and enjoys robustness to outlying observations. It is based on an iterative clipping procedure over expanding Mahalanobis balls, or ``balloons.'' The method satisfies zero-concentrated differential privacy and depends on a small number of interpretable tuning parameters. We provide theoretical guarantees under heavy-tailed and contaminated elliptical models, characterizing its statistical performance and robustness to outliers. Extensive simulations demonstrate that the balloon mean is robust to heavy-tailed and contaminated data, and outperforms existing differentially private mean estimators in contaminated settings.

</details>


### [233] [Restricted Multivariate Spatial Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12677)
*Jihyeon Kwon,Harrison Quick*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种量化多元条件自回归（MCAR）模型信息量的框架，并引入一种通过重新参数化来控制其信息量的方法，以确保模型对各子群的贡献具有可比性，避免过度精确的估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: BYM CAR模型在小区域健康事件建模中被广泛使用，但已有多项研究表明其可能过于"信息丰富"（overly informative），导致估计过度精确。MCAR模型作为其多元扩展，理论上因借用跨子群的额外信息而更具信息量，但其信息量水平此前从未被量化。作者旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 作者提出了一套测量MCAR模型信息量的框架（作为先前工作的扩展），并通过对MCAR模型进行重新参数化，在一个计算高效的框架内控制模型的信息贡献，确保各子群获得可比较的模型贡献。

Result: 研究展示了MCAR模型与BYM CAR模型在信息量和过度平滑方面的比较，并利用按种族和性别分层的县级心脏病死亡数据，展示了受约束MCAR模型的优势。

Conclusion: 受约束的MCAR模型能够在借用跨子群信息的同时，有效控制模型的信息量，避免过度平滑和过度精确的估计，优于传统的BYM CAR和未约束的MCAR模型。

Abstract: When modeling health events in small areas, the conditional autoregressive (CAR) framework of Besag, York, and Mollié (BYM) is widely used. For multiple outcomes, the multivariate CAR (MCAR) extension accommodates dependence among diseases that share risk factors, in addition to spatial dependence, and can also jointly model demographic subgroups for a single disease, allowing information to be borrowed across related populations. However, recent studies have shown that the BYM CAR model can be overly informative, leading to excessively precise estimates. While the MCAR model is expected to be more informative due to additional information shared across subgroups, its level of informativeness has not been previously quantified. We propose a framework to measure MCAR model informativeness as an extension of prior work and introduce a method to control it, ensuring the model contributes comparably to each subgroup. We achieve this through a reparameterization of the MCAR model within a computationally efficient framework. We demonstrate how the MCAR model compares with the BYM CAR model in terms of informativeness and oversmoothing and highlight the advantages of the restricted MCAR model using county-level heart disease death data stratified by race and sex.

</details>


### [234] [Bayesian machine learning approach for recurrent events studies using Soft Bayesian Additive Regression Trees (SBART)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12701)
*MengXing Chen,Debajyoti Sinha,Antonio Linero*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文提出RecSBART方法，将Soft Bayesian Additive Regression Trees用于复发事件数据的非参数建模，通过结合软决策树与两层数据增强策略，实现复发强度函数的高精度估计与复杂协变量关系的揭示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生物医学研究中复发事件数据常见，但现有方法难以同时实现高预测精度和对协变量非线性效应、交互作用的灵活捕捉。需要一个兼具预测性能与可解释性的非参数建模框架。

Method: 将非齐次泊松过程的条件强度函数分解为时间常数基线、个体脆弱性随机效应和SBART非参数成分三部分的乘积。SBART成分基于软决策树的贝叶斯集成学习，捕获协变量与时间的非线性效应及交互。采用两层数据增强方案高效融入SBART组件进行后验计算。

Result: 模拟研究表明，即使建模假设不完全成立，RecSBART在累积强度估计方面仍优于现有方法。在结直肠癌患者复发住院的贝叶斯分析中，进一步展示了该方法揭示和解释复发事件数据中复杂协变量关系的能力。

Conclusion: RecSBART作为一种贝叶斯非参数集成学习方法，为复发事件分析提供了一个灵活、准确且可解释的框架，在理论假设偏离时仍具鲁棒性，适合生物医学复发数据建模。

Abstract: Recurrent event data frequently arise in biomedical studies, where individuals may experience multiple recurrences of the same type of events, such as recurrent hospitalizations. This article introduces a nonparametric method for recurrent events under a Bayesian ensemble learning framework, called Soft Bayesian Additive Regression Trees (SBART), which combines multiple soft decision trees to achieve high predictive accuracy and a smooth estimator of the underlying intensity of the recurrent events. The proposed model represents the conditional intensity function of the non-homogeneous Poisson process as the product of a time-constant baseline, a subject-specific frailty random effect, and a nonparametric component capturing potentially nonlinear covariate effects and unknown interactions among covariates and time. A two-layer data augmentation scheme is employed to efficiently incorporate the SBART component within our computational algorithm. Simulation studies demonstrate that our method, called RecSBART in short, achieves superior accuracy in estimating cumulative intensity compared to existing approaches, even when our modeling assumptions are not true. With the Bayesian analysis of a study of recurrent hospitalizations of colorectal cancer patients, we further demonstrate our RecSBART method's ability to reveal and interpret the underlying complex relationships among covariates in a recurrent events study.

</details>


### [235] [Discrepancy Modeling with Intermediate Variables: A New Framework for Robust Gaussian Process Calibration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12857)
*Henry Shaowu Yuchi,Michael Grosskopf,Aman Sharma,Nicolas Schunck,Jared O'Neal,Matt Menickelly,Stefan M. Wild*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 提出一种鲁棒的高斯过程校准框架，通过利用中间变量进行偏差建模，集成结构化中间变量选择、离散化缩放高斯随机过程（S-GaSP）约束偏差项，以及空间填充设计策略，实现仿真器与偏差的联合建模，从而提升预测性能、提供有原则的不确定性量化，并缓解可识别性问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准高斯过程校准框架忽略了计算机实验产生的大量中间变量；单独拟合仿真器和偏差模型会引发可识别性问题；需要一种能充分利用中间变量、联合建模仿真器与偏差的鲁棒校准方法。

Method: 提出一个三管齐下的框架：(1) 结构化中间变量选择过程，筛选对偏差建模有用的中间变量；(2) 使用离散化缩放高斯随机过程（S-GaSP）作为偏差项的约束先验，控制偏差的复杂度；(3) 采用空间填充设计策略选取约束点（constraint points），确保偏差建模的覆盖性。三者集成实现仿真器与偏差的联合建模。

Result: 在核物理结合能应用上验证，所提框架在预测性能上优于基线方法，并提供有原则的不确定性量化，同时缓解了可识别性风险。

Conclusion: 该框架通过系统性地利用中间变量来建模偏差，成功解决了分离拟合带来的可识别性问题，实现了仿真器与偏差的鲁棒联合建模，在预测精度和不确定性量化方面均优于传统方法。

Abstract: Gaussian processes are widely used for surrogate modeling in computer experiments, which often produce numerous intermediate variables that are not explicitly used in standard calibration frameworks. Calibration of imperfect models can be challenging without leveraging these variables, while fitting the emulator and the discrepancy models separately also poses identifiability issues. In this work, we propose a robust Gaussian process calibration framework that leverages intermediate variables for discrepancy modeling. The framework integrates a structured intermediate variable selection process, a discretized scaled Gaussian stochastic process (S-GaSP) to constrain the discrepancy term, and a space-filling design strategy for selecting constraint points. This enables joint modeling of the emulator and discrepancy, improving predictive performance, providing principled uncertainty quantification, and alleviating identifiability risks. We demonstrate its efficacy on a nuclear physics application involving binding energies, where it outperforms baseline approaches.

</details>


### [236] [Volterra--Wiener--Kunchenko Orthogonalization: From Wiener--Hermite to Distribution-Matched Volterra Bases](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12884)
*Serhii Zabolotnii*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 针对有限记忆Volterra辨识在非高斯输入下的病态问题，本文通过定向Gram-Schmidt正交化在L²(P)空间构造分布匹配的VWK基，作为任意多项式混沌坐标系。核心贡献包括：二阶误设惩罚定理（揭示偏度系数对超量风险的控制）、条件数实验验证VWK基的数值优势，以及Lean 4形式化证明Krawtchouk恒等式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 有限记忆Volterra辨识中，单项式参数化在非高斯输入下呈现严重病态；Wiener-Hermite展开仅在高斯白噪声输入下能消除该病态。亟需一种针对任意输入分布的、良条件的正交基构造方法。

Method: 1. 在L²(P)空间中对单项式进行定向Gram-Schmidt正交化，构造分布匹配的Volterra-Wiener-Kunchenko (VWK)基，作为任意多项式混沌坐标系（延续Xiu & Karniadakis的广义多项式混沌和Oladyshkin & Nowak的数据驱动任意多项式混沌）。2. 推导二阶误设惩罚定理，分析方差匹配高斯基下自归一化对角估计量的超量L²(P)风险。3. 进行条件数实验，比较总体匹配Gram矩阵与有限样本设计Gram矩阵。4. 用Lean 4形式化证明任意N的Binomial(N,p) Krawtchouk行恒等式。

Result: 1. 理论结果：超量L²(P)风险由偏度系数δ=μ₃/σ²控制，对称输入下精确消失。2. 数值结果：n=2000时，中心化指数分布的经验VWK Gram条件数远优于幂基Gram，但随多项式阶数升高而退化。3. 形式化验证：Lean 4成功证明Binomial(N,p)下的Krawtchouk行恒等式。4. 满秩最小二乘是基不变的，VWK的优势在于稳定对角互相关和正则化坐标拟合，而非声称普遍预测优越性。

Conclusion: VWK基通过匹配输入分布构造正交坐标系，有效缓解非高斯输入下Volterra辨识的病态问题。其优势主要体现在对角估计的稳定性和正则化场景，而非满秩最小二乘。方法受限于矩框架、有限记忆和乘积输入律假设。

Abstract: The monomial parameterization of finite-memory Volterra identification is ill-conditioned under non-Gaussian input, and the Wiener--Hermite expansion removes this ill-conditioning only for Gaussian white-noise input. We construct the distribution-matched Volterra--Wiener--Kunchenko (VWK) basis by oriented Gram--Schmidt orthogonalization of monomials in $L^2(P)$ and use it as an arbitrary-polynomial-chaos coordinate system for finite-memory Volterra identification from data, following the generalized polynomial chaos of Xiu and Karniadakis (2002) and the data-driven arbitrary polynomial chaos of Oladyshkin and Nowak (2012). The basis itself is classical; the contribution is the Volterra-estimation reading. First, an order-2 misspecification-penalty theorem shows that a self-normalized diagonal estimator in the variance-matched Gaussian basis incurs an excess $L^2(P)$ risk governed by the skew coefficient $δ=μ_3/σ^2$, vanishing exactly for symmetric inputs. Second, conditioning experiments separate the constructional fact that the population matched Gram is the identity from the finite-sample design Gram: at $n=2000$, the centered-exponential empirical VWK Gram remains far better conditioned than the power Gram, although it degrades with degree. Third, a machine-checked Lean 4 proof establishes the Binomial$(N,p)$ Krawtchouk row for arbitrary $N$. Full least squares over a fixed span is basis-invariant, so VWK stabilizes diagonal cross-correlation and regularized coordinate fits rather than claiming universal prediction superiority. The analysis is moment-based, finite-memory, and restricted to product input laws.

</details>


### [237] [Diagnostics-guided variance-inflated Fay-Herriot estimation from non-probability samples](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13025)
*Andrius Čiginas*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 针对非概率数据源在小域估计中的可靠性差异问题，本文提出了一种诊断引导的方差膨胀Fay-Herriot(FH)估计量。该方法从校准的逆概率加权(IPW)域估计出发，通过域诊断指标识别IPW信息薄弱的域，并在FH观测方程中引入混合方差膨胀分量，使得诊断结果较差的域更强地向回归均值收缩，从而有效降低估计误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非概率样本虽广泛用于小域估计，但IPW产生的域估计量可靠性在不同域之间差异显著。标准FH模型将输入的域水平方差估计视为对输入估计量不确定性的完整刻画，然而当某些域存在覆盖不足、权重不稳定或辅助变量平衡差等问题时，这些特征所暗示的选择偏差风险并未被估计方差所捕获。因此需要一种方法，将域诊断信息纳入平滑过程，以区分对待可靠性不同的域。

Method: 提出诊断引导的方差膨胀FH估计量：首先从校准的IPW域估计量出发；其次通过一小组域诊断指标（涉及覆盖性、权重稳定性、辅助平衡等）量化各域的IPW信息可靠性；然后在FH观测方程中引入混合方差膨胀分量——诊断结果较差的域获得更大的方差膨胀，从而在分层贝叶斯框架下被更强地收缩至域水平回归均值，实现自适应平滑。

Result: 基于立陶宛企业伪真实总体的已知真值验证表明，所提方法相较于校准IPW，在有限总体域总值的估计误差上有显著降低。

Conclusion: 将域诊断与方差膨胀机制嵌入FH模型，能够有效识别并缓解非概率样本中选择偏差对域估计的影响，实现更可靠的平滑。该方法为结合非概率数据源的小域估计提供了实用且稳健的改进方案。

Abstract: Non-probability data sources are increasingly considered in small area estimation, but inverse probability weighting (IPW) gives model-dependent domain estimators whose reliability may vary substantially across domains. Standard Fay-Herriot (FH) smoothing borrows strength across domains, yet it uses the supplied area-level variance estimates as if they fully described the uncertainty of the input estimators. This can be misleading when some domains have weak coverage, unstable weights, or poor auxiliary balance, since these features may indicate selection-bias risk not captured by the estimated variance alone. We propose a diagnostics-guided variance-inflated FH estimator for finite-population domain totals. The method starts from calibrated IPW domain estimators, summarizes their reliability through a small set of domain diagnostics, and introduces a mixture variance-inflation component in the FH observation equation. Domains whose diagnostics indicate weaker IPW information are thereby smoothed more strongly toward the area-level regression mean. A truth-known validation based on a pseudo-real population of Lithuanian business enterprises shows a substantial reduction in estimation error relative to calibrated IPW.

</details>


### [238] [Calibrating simplified vine copulas with a noise contrastive estimation approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13213)
*Michael Denis Kraus,David Huk,Claudia Czado*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于噪声对比估计（NCE）的简化Vine Copula模型校正策略。通过将已拟合的简化Vine Copula作为噪声模型，利用NCE为每个观测样本生成校正因子，从而在局部调整简化模型以逼近真实的数据依赖结构。该方法在简化假设被违反时能有效提升模型精度，在假设成立时则保持中性，且不牺牲计算可处理性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Vine Copula的简化假设（条件pair copula独立于条件值）虽极大便利了估计，但在条件依赖关系变化显著的应用中会导致模型误设。研究者希望在保留简化Vine Copula计算可处理性的前提下，对其局部偏差进行校正。

Method: 将已拟合的简化Vine Copula作为噪声分布，运用噪声对比估计（NCE）将密度估计重构为二分类任务，为每个观测样本导出校正因子，从而获得修正后的对数似然估计，实现对简化Vine的局部调整。

Result: 模拟研究表明：当简化假设被违反时，所提校正方法能提供合理且有效的调整以提升模型精度；当简化模型本身已充分时，方法保持中性。两个实际数据应用进一步展示了其实用价值。

Conclusion: 基于NCE的校正策略是一种有前景的工具，能在不放弃计算可处理性的前提下增强简化Vine Copula模型的表现。

Abstract: Vine copulas provide a flexible framework for modeling complex multivariate dependence structures using only bivariate building blocks. Their practical success relies heavily on the simplifying assumption, which restricts conditional pair copulas to be independent of the specific conditioning values. While this assumption greatly facilitates estimation, it may lead to model misspecification in applications with pronounced varying conditional dependence. We propose a novel calibration strategy for simplified vine copula models based on observation-specific correction factors. These factors are derived using noise contrastive estimation (NCE), a supervised learning technique for density estimation that reframes the problem as a binary classification task with an easily sampled noise distribution. Treating the fitted simplified vine copula as the noise model, the NCE approach yields corrected log-likelihood estimates for individual observations, thereby locally adjusting the simplified vine toward the underlying data-generating dependence structure. Simulation studies demonstrate that the proposed calibration provides sensible and effective adjustments, improving model accuracy when the simplifying assumption is violated while remaining neutral when the simplified model is adequate. Two real-data applications further illustrate the practical benefits of the method. The results highlight NCE-based calibration as a promising tool to enhance simplified vine copula models without abandoning their computational tractability.

</details>


### [239] [Least Absolute Deviations Estimation for Sinusoidal Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13242)
*Zehaan Naik,Debasis Kundu*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于最小绝对偏差（LAD）的鲁棒正弦回归模型参数估计方法，采用坐标下降算法交替更新幅度参数（加权中位数）和频率参数（周期图谱搜索+局部精化），在理论上证明了一致性和渐近正态性，并在合成及实际时间序列数据上验证了其对非高斯噪声的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典正弦回归参数估计主要依赖最小二乘方法，但最小二乘对重尾噪声和异常值极为敏感。实际时间序列数据（如大气CO₂、航空客流等）常存在非高斯噪声，亟需一种鲁棒且计算高效的替代方案。

Method: 作者将估计问题建模为LAD目标的直接最小化，提出一种模块化坐标下降算法。该算法利用目标函数的部分凸性：幅度参数通过加权中位数计算更新（大幅优于传统的单纯形优化方法），频率参数则采用基于周期图谱思想的网格搜索辅以局部精化。理论上，在温和正则条件下证明了估计量的强一致性和渐近正态性。

Result: 合成数据集实验表明，在重尾噪声和异常值场景下，所提方法显著优于最小二乘法。在三个真实数据集（Mauna Loa大气CO₂数据、航空旅客数据、英国驾驶员死亡数据）上，方法展现出对非高斯噪声的有效鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 该方法为 sinusoidal 信号估计提供了一个简洁、可解释且鲁棒的替代方案，在计算效率（利用加权中位数避免单纯形方法）和统计性质上均有良好表现，是对传统最小二乘框架的有益补充。

Abstract: We study robust parameter estimation in sinusoidal regression models within a least absolute deviations (LAD) framework. While classical approaches rely predominantly on least-squares formulations, they are known to be sensitive to heavy-tailed noise and outliers.
